## Best date plans

### 2026-09-12 — Stew: AIR 17 Final Exhibition regular gallery hours + Winner on Franklin

**Stew: AIR 17 Final Exhibition regular gallery hours, then a meal.** Bakery and cafe at Winner on Franklin. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.

The park's 1912 carousel operates with carved horses, lions, giraffes and deer; tickets are sold per ride or in five-ride books.

**Practical note:** Best as a short add-on to a park walk rather than a full outing.

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 47.9  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.
[Official](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

## Where to eat

A concise September dining update with old heatmap entries downgraded from “new,” duplicate generic menus removed, and exact deals kept only where restaurant-specific sources support them.

Dining research: 2026-09-10T10:35:45+00:00

No candidate is given an NYC Michelin star claim. The only Michelin-related language found was Eater background context about the Texas restaurants behind Kirbee’s and El Califa de León’s Mexico City original; those are not treated as awards for the NYC candidates. Several official restaurant pages rendered only shells, so captured HTML/JSON-LD and linked PDFs were used where clearer.

### Cote 550 brings Cote’s Korean steakhouse format to Midtown

More restaurants — Cote 550

Eater’s September 2026 Manhattan heatmap says Simon Kim opened the second Cote location as part of the 550 Madison project in April, with a more mature version of the Butcher’s Feast and steak omakase.

Conditions: Eater reports Cote 550 opened in April 2026, so this should be treated as an established restaurant lead rather than a current September opening. Exact opening day was not verified on the official page. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#cote-550  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#cote-550)

### Elder opens as a British Indian chophouse in Hudson Yards

New openings — Elder

Eater reports Chicago chef Sujan Sarkar expanded his NYC footprint after Indienne with Elder, serving spiced chops such as Tellicherry peppercorn steak and lamb chops with dry burrah spice plus vegetable dishes.

Conditions: Eater reports opening in August 2026; exact day not verified on official site.  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#elder)

### Muje debuts Jungsik Yim’s playful Korean tasting-menu spot

New openings — Muje

Eater says Jungsik Yim opened Muje in July in a former space, with an eight-course menu crossing Asian dining styles; useful for a splurge tasting-menu choice distinct from Jungsik.

Conditions: Eater reports July 2026 opening; official page did not expose menu price through fetch.  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#muje)

### Ambassadors Clubhouse serves Punjabi cooking in NoMad

More restaurants — Ambassadors Clubhouse

Eater says the Sethi siblings opened the two-story NoMad restaurant in February, with chef Karan Mittal’s menu spanning papads, chaats, chutneys, kebabs, biryani, matka, and karahi dishes.

Conditions: Eater reports a February 2026 opening, and the official page confirms Punjabi/North Indian culinary focus; do not frame it as a new September opening. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#ambassadors-clubhouse and https://ambassadorsclubhouse.com/newyork/  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#ambassadors-clubhouse)

### Arthur is a Greenpoint bistro from Kevin and Alexa Finch

More restaurants — Arthur

Eater says Arthur opened in April in the former Fulgurances space with bistro-meets-New American dishes such as snail skewers, beef tartare, steak, unusual wines by the glass, and cocktails including a brioche martini.

Conditions: Eater reports Arthur opened in April 2026, so classify as a restaurant rather than a current September opening. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap#arthur  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap#arthur)

### Sailor & Siren becomes a full Greenpoint seafood shack

New openings — Sailor & Siren

Eater says the former lobster-roll pop-up opened as a full restaurant in early July, serving Maine lobster rolls with optional caviar or uni, hot dogs from Prospect Butcher Co., whoopie pies, and pocket pies.

Schedule: Friday-Sunday 12pm until sold out or 8pm  
Conditions: Eater reports a full-restaurant opening in early July 2026; the official site confirms the Greenpoint address as 817A Manhattan Ave and public hours Friday-Sunday from noon until sold out or 8pm. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap#sailor-siren and https://sailorandsirenshack.com/  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap#sailor-siren)

### Kirbee’s brings two Texas barbecue teams to Greenpoint

New openings — Kirbee’s

Eater reports Barbs B Q’s Chuck Charnichart and Goldee’s Jonny White opened Kirbee’s with two barbecue points of view: South Texas/Mexican-spiced brisket, lime-zest ribs, charro beans, green spaghetti, plus classic brisket, ribs, turkey, potato salad, and coleslaw.

Schedule: Daytime/lunch barbecue format; first day 11:00-14:00, full opening Labor Day weekend 2026 per Eater  
Conditions: Opened Sunday 2026-08-30 with limited 11am-2pm service, then a full Labor Day weekend opening Friday 2026-09-04 through Sunday 2026-09-06; ongoing post-opening hours were not verified. Source: https://ny.eater.com/news/413426/kirbees-nyc-barbecue-restaurant-open-august-2026-barbs-b-q-goldees-chichuck-charnichart-jonny-white  
From: 2026-08-30  
Opened: 2026-08-30  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413426/kirbees-nyc-barbecue-restaurant-open-august-2026-barbs-b-q-goldees-chichuck-charnichart-jonny-white)

### Burmese Bites opens a full-service Astoria restaurant

New openings — Burmese Bites

Eater says chef-owner Myo Lin Thway transformed the former Point Brazil into a full-service restaurant, carrying over street-festival and Queens Night Market favorites such as palatas, tea leaf salad, ohno kauswe, mohinga, and crispy anchovy chile fries.

Conditions: New to Eater’s September 2026 Queens heatmap; exact opening day was not stated in the fetched text.  
Published: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#burmese-bites)

### Jin Broth serves Korean seolleongtang inside Marquee Astoria

More restaurants — Jin Broth

Eater says Jin Broth opened in May as a takeout counter inside Marquee Astoria, focused on long-simmered Korean seolleongtang with scallions, salt, and pepper, plus suyuk and dumplings described as lasagna-style.

Conditions: Eater says Jin Broth opened in May 2026 as a takeout counter inside Marquee Astoria; classify as a restaurant/takeout counter lead, not a current September opening. Official page confirmed only broad broth-focused positioning. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#jin-broth and https://www.jinbroth.com/  
Published: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#jin-broth)

### Xiaozhan Rice Noodle serves clay-pot rice noodle soups in Flushing

More restaurants — Xiaozhan Rice Noodle

Eater says Xiaozhan opened in May and serves ten mini clay-pot rice noodle soups with fish, beef, shrimp, or chicken, multiple soup bases, and a sauce bar; go off-peak because the tiny space can have long waits.

Conditions: Eater says Xiaozhan opened in May 2026, so classify as a restaurant rather than a current September opening. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#xiaozhan-rice-noodle  
Published: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#xiaozhan-rice-noodle)

### Dagon weekday lunch mezze combo

Deals & happy hours — Dagon

Official menu lists a weekday lunch window and a mezze section priced as a shareable set, useful for an affordable Upper West Side sit-down lunch.

Price: Choice of 3 mezze at $27 or 6 mezze at $47; individual mezze listed at $10  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 11:30am-3pm  
Conditions: Official online menu lists lunch Monday-Friday 11:30am-3pm and mezze choice of 3 for $27 or 6 for $47, with individual mezze at $10; page notes sample PDFs and says to use online menus for current information. Source: https://www.dagonnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.dagonnyc.com/menus/)

### Crown Shy lists lunch prix-fixe availability downtown

Special menus — Crown Shy

Official menu page states a lunch menu with a prix fixe menu available, useful for a business-district set-menu option at a fine-dining-adjacent restaurant.

Price: Prix-fixe price unknown; a la carte lunch items checked include chicken kofta $27, Ora King salmon $29, pork katsu sandwich $29, rigatoni $34  
Schedule: Lunch schedule not stated on fetched menu page  
Conditions: Official menu text says “Prix Fixe Menu Available,” but the fetched official text did not expose the prix-fixe price or course structure; keep only as availability, with a la carte lunch prices separately sourced. Source: https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Canton Harbor brings Cantonese banquet-hall dining to Long Island City

More restaurants — Canton Harbor

Eater’s September 2026 Queens heatmap says a Flushing restaurant opened a Long Island City location of Canton Harbor; useful as a new Cantonese lead, but the fetched source chunk did not expose a street address.

Price: Weekday lunch specials $14-$17, served with soup and rice, per Eater  
Conditions: Eater reports Canton Harbor opened in Long Island City and identifies weekday lunch specials at $14-$17; exact opening date, official URL, and hours were not verified. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#canton-harbor  
Published: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap#canton-harbor)

### Ugly Baby returns in Williamsburg after a 20-month closure

New openings — Ugly Baby

Eater’s September 2026 Brooklyn heatmap says Ugly Baby returned and was newly added to the Brooklyn heatmap; useful for Thai-food watchers, but exact address and reopening date were not captured in fetched text.

Conditions: Eater reports Ugly Baby relocated to Williamsburg and reopened in late August 2026 after a 20-month shutter; exact official hours and reservation needs were not verified. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap#ugly-baby  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap#ugly-baby)

### Hawksmoor New York weekday set lunch tiers

Deals & happy hours — Hawksmoor New York

Official location page advertises a British midweek lunch with mix-and-match 1-, 2-, or 3-course pricing and names dishes including steak frites, fish and chips, potted beef and bacon, and Coronation salad.

Price: 1 course $25; 2 courses $40; 3 courses $55; steak frites noted at $25  
Schedule: Location page: lunch Monday-Saturday 12:00pm-3:00pm; kitchen lunch Monday-Saturday 12:00pm-3:00pm  
Conditions: Official location page lists the midweek lunch special at 1 course $25, 2 courses $40, or 3 courses $55, with Steak Frites at $25; same official location page lists lunch/kitchen Monday-Saturday 12pm-3pm, while an older lunch-offerings page says Wed-Sat 12-3, so day coverage should be rechecked before going. Source: https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/ and https://thehawksmoor.com/us/hawksmoor-lunch-offerings/  
[Source](https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/)

### Red Rooster Harlem weekday lunch prix fixe

Deals & happy hours — Red Rooster Harlem

Official lunch-specials PDF lists a two-course lunch prix fixe with first-course choices such as sweet potato coconut soup or Farmer’s Remix salad and second-course choices such as Yardbird chicken or pan-fried catfish.

Price: $35 lunch prix fixe  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 12pm-4pm  
Conditions: PDF states add-ons including fried or grilled chicken +$10, jerk salmon or shrimp +$12, lobster +$19, and white meat +$12. Tax, tip, and service charges not stated.  
[Source](https://www.redroosterharlem.com/s/Lunch-Specials-2025docx-3.pdf)

### Beatstro Bronx brunch unlimited mimosa and sangria add-on

Deals & happy hours — Beatstro

Official brunch-menu page lists a 90-minute unlimited Bomb Sangria and mimosa option, adding a concrete Bronx deal to the guide.

Price: $24.95 per person  
Schedule: Brunch page does not state brunch-service hours; site schema lists Beatstro opening at 11:30am Saturday and Sunday, but not that brunch runs for the full operating day.  
Conditions: Official brunch page confirms “90 MIN UNLIMITED BOMB SANGRIA & MIMOSA - $24.95 Per Person” and “GRATUITY ADDED TO ALL PARTIES”; food-purchase requirement and tax were not stated. Source: https://www.beatstro.com/brunch-menu

## Discovery coverage

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: New York Comedy Club 11/38; Brooklyn 10/38. Brooklyn missed the 40% target because late-week official Brooklyn theater, museum, and comedy calendars were blocked or did not expose dated listings; retained Brooklyn items were only those with verifiable public dates.
